           │ ```python                                                    │
           │ class MutualExclusion(Module):                               │
           │     def locals(self):                                        │
           │         self.s = BitVector(1)                                │
           │         self.y0 = BitVector(1)                               │
           │         self.y1 = BitVector(1)                               │

           │         self.s = BitVectorVal(1, 1)                          │
           │         self.y0 = BitVectorVal(0, 1) #34                     │
           │         self.y1 = BitVectorVal(0, 1) #41                     │
           │                                                              │
           │     def next(self):                                          │
           │         self.y0 = self.s                                     │
           │         await self.y1 == 0                                   │
           │         self.s = self.y1                                     │
           │         await self.y0 == 0                                   │
           │         self.y1 = self.s                                     │
           │                                                              │
           │     def specification(self):                                 │
           │         self.s_inv = (self.s == 0) | (self.s == 1)           │
           │         self.y0_inv = (self.y0 == 0) | (self.y0 == 1)        │
           │         self.y1_inv = (self.y1 == 0) | (self.y1 == 1)        │
           │         self.s_inv_init = (self.s == 1)                      │

           │         self.y1_inv_init = (self.y1 == 0)                    │
           │         self.s_inv_P0 = (self.s == 0)                        │
           │         self.s_inv_P1 = (self.s == 1)                        │

           │ i)); l4: Critical section l5: yi := 0 end.  Here, the        │
           │ statement (yi, s) := (1, i); is a multiple assignment in     │
           │ which variable yi := 1 and s := i is a single, atomic step.  │

           │ that checks whether the algorithm ensures mutual exclusion   │

           │ two processes due to Pnueli. There is a single shared        │

           │ each process has a local Boolean variable y that initially   │

           │ statement (yi, s) := (1, i); is a multiple assignment in     │
